import { Tabs } from 'expo-router';
import { Home, Users, Landmark, Banknote, Menu } from 'lucide-react-native';

export default function TabLayout() {
  return (
    <Tabs screenOptions={{ 
      tabBarActiveTintColor: '#14283F',
      tabBarInactiveTintColor: '#64748B',
      headerStyle: {
        backgroundColor: '#14283F',
      },
      headerTintColor: '#FFFFFF',
    }}>
      <Tabs.Screen
        name="index"
        options={{
          title: 'Home',
          // @ts-ignore
          tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <Home color={color as string} size={24} />,
        }}
      />
      <Tabs.Screen
        name="borrowers"
        options={{
          title: 'Borrowers',
          // @ts-ignore
          tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <Users color={color as string} size={24} />,
        }}
      />
      <Tabs.Screen
        name="loans"
        options={{
          title: 'Loans',
          // @ts-ignore
          tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <Landmark color={color as string} size={24} />,
        }}
      />
      <Tabs.Screen
        name="collections"
        options={{
          title: 'Collections',
          // @ts-ignore
          tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <Banknote color={color as string} size={24} />,
        }}
      />
      <Tabs.Screen
        name="more"
        options={{
          title: 'More',
          // @ts-ignore
          tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <Menu color={color as string} size={24} />,
        }}
      />
    </Tabs>
  );
}

import { Stack } from 'expo-router';
import { DatabaseProvider } from '../core/database/DatabaseProvider';
import { PinLock } from '../ui/PinLock';

import { SafeAreaProvider } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';

export default function RootLayout() {
  return (
    <SafeAreaProvider>
      <DatabaseProvider>
        <PinLock>
          <Stack>
            <Stack.Screen name="(tabs)" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
            <Stack.Screen name="borrower" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
            <Stack.Screen name="loan" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
          </Stack>
        </PinLock>
      </DatabaseProvider>
    </SafeAreaProvider>
  );
}

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback, useMemo } from 'react';
import { View, FlatList, StyleSheet, Alert, Modal, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity, TextInput } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { Typography } from '../../ui/Typography';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../../ui/Card';
import { Input } from '../../ui/Input';
import { theme } from '../../ui/theme';
import { useDatabase } from '../../core/database/DatabaseProvider';
import { Loan } from '../../core/database/loanRepository';
import { Borrower } from '../../core/database/borrowerRepository';
import { Paise, toPaise, formatINR } from '../../core/domain/money';
import { generateSchedule } from '../../core/domain/schedule';

export default function LoansScreen() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { loans, borrowers, refreshVersion, triggerRefresh } = useDatabase();
  const [data, setData] = useState<Loan[]>([]);
  const [borrowersList, setBorrowersList] = useState<Borrower[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  
  // New Loan Wizard State
  const [isModalVisible, setModalVisible] = useState(false);
  const [step, setStep] = useState(1);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  
  // Form State
  const [selectedBorrowerId, setSelectedBorrowerId] = useState('');
  const [productType, setProductType] = useState<'instalment' | 'bullet'>('instalment');
  const [principal, setPrincipal] = useState('10000');
  const [markupPercent, setMarkupPercent] = useState('30');
  const [termMonths, setTermMonths] = useState('6');
  const [dueDay, setDueDay] = useState('5');
  const [collateral, setCollateral] = useState('');

  const loadData = useCallback(async () => {
    if (loans && borrowers) {
      try {
        setError(null);
        setData(await loans.getAll());
        setBorrowersList(await borrowers.getAll());
      } catch (err: any) {
        setError(err.message || 'Failed to load loans');
      }
    }
  }, [loans, borrowers]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    loadData();
  }, [loadData, refreshVersion]);

  const filteredBorrowers = useMemo(() => {
    if (!searchQuery) return borrowersList;
    const lower = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    return borrowersList.filter(b => 
      b.name.toLowerCase().includes(lower) || 
      b.phone.includes(lower)
    );
  }, [borrowersList, searchQuery]);

  const previewSchedule = useMemo(() => {
    if (step === 4) {
      try {
        return generateSchedule({
          principal: toPaise(parseInt(principal) || 0),
          markupFactor: (parseFloat(markupPercent) || 0) / 100,
          termMonths: parseInt(termMonths) || 1,
          startDate: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
          dueDay: parseInt(dueDay) || 1,
          productType
        });
      } catch (e) {
        return [];
      }
    }
    return [];
  }, [step, principal, markupPercent, termMonths, dueDay, productType]);

  const totalRepayment = useMemo(() => {
    const sum = previewSchedule.reduce((acc, kist) => acc + (kist.amountDue as unknown as number), 0);
    return sum as unknown as Paise;
  }, [previewSchedule]);

  const handleCreate = async () => {
    if (!selectedBorrowerId) return;
    try {
      await loans!.createLoanWithSchedule(selectedBorrowerId, {
        principal: toPaise(parseInt(principal)),
        markupFactor: parseFloat(markupPercent) / 100,
        termMonths: parseInt(termMonths),
        startDate: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
        dueDay: parseInt(dueDay),
        productType,
      });
      setModalVisible(false);
      resetForm();
      triggerRefresh(); // Automatically triggers re-fetch via useEffect
    } catch (e: any) {
      Alert.alert('Error', e.message);
    }
  };

  const resetForm = () => {
    setStep(1);
    setSelectedBorrowerId('');
    setProductType('instalment');
    setPrincipal('10000');
    setMarkupPercent('30');
    setTermMonths('6');
    setDueDay('5');
    setCollateral('');
    setSearchQuery('');
  };

  const getBorrowerName = (id: string) => {
    return borrowersList.find(b => b.id === id)?.name || 'Unknown Borrower';
  };

  const renderStep1 = () => (
    <View style={styles.stepContainer}>
      <Typography variant="headlineMd" style={styles.modalTitle}>Step 1: Borrower & Product</Typography>
      <Input
        label="Search Borrower"
        placeholder="Search borrowers by name or phone..."
        value={searchQuery}
        onChangeText={setSearchQuery}
      />
      <FlatList
        style={styles.borrowerList}
        data={filteredBorrowers}
        keyExtractor={item => item.id}
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <TouchableOpacity 
            style={[styles.borrowerCard, selectedBorrowerId === item.id && styles.borrowerCardSelected]}
            onPress={() => setSelectedBorrowerId(item.id)}
          >
            <Typography variant="headlineSm">{item.name}</Typography>
            <Typography variant="bodySm" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>{item.phone}</Typography>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      />
      
      <Typography variant="labelLg" style={{marginTop: theme.spacing.lg}}>Select Product</Typography>
      <View style={styles.productToggle}>
        <TouchableOpacity 
          style={[styles.productBtn, productType === 'instalment' && styles.productBtnActive]}
          onPress={() => setProductType('instalment')}
        >
          <Typography color={productType === 'instalment' ? '#fff' : theme.colors.primary}>Upfront Markup</Typography>
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <TouchableOpacity 
          style={[styles.productBtn, productType === 'bullet' && styles.productBtnActive]}
          onPress={() => setProductType('bullet')}
        >
          <Typography color={productType === 'bullet' ? '#fff' : theme.colors.primary}>Monthly Bullet</Typography>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <Button title="Next: Terms" onPress={() => setStep(2)} disabled={!selectedBorrowerId} style={styles.nextBtn} />
    </View>
  );

  const renderStep2 = () => (
    <View style={styles.stepContainer}>
      <Typography variant="headlineMd" style={styles.modalTitle}>Step 2: Terms & Calculation</Typography>
      <Input label="Principal (â‚¹)" keyboardType="numeric" value={principal} onChangeText={setPrincipal} />
      <Input label="Markup Percentage (%) (e.g. 30 for 30%)" keyboardType="numeric" value={markupPercent} onChangeText={setMarkupPercent} />
      <Input label="Tenure (Months)" keyboardType="numeric" value={termMonths} onChangeText={setTermMonths} />
      <Input label="Due Day (1-31)" keyboardType="numeric" value={dueDay} onChangeText={setDueDay} />
      
      <View style={styles.row}>
        <Button title="Back" variant="outline" onPress={() => setStep(1)} style={{flex: 1, marginRight: 8}} />
        <Button title="Next: Security" onPress={() => setStep(3)} style={{flex: 1}} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );

  const renderStep3 = () => (
    <View style={styles.stepContainer}>
      <Typography variant="headlineMd" style={styles.modalTitle}>Step 3: Security & Collateral</Typography>
      <Input label="Collateral Details (Optional)" value={collateral} onChangeText={setCollateral} />
      
      <View style={styles.row}>
        <Button title="Back" variant="outline" onPress={() => setStep(2)} style={{flex: 1, marginRight: 8}} />
        <Button title="Next: Review" onPress={() => setStep(4)} style={{flex: 1}} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );

  const renderStep4 = () => (
    <ScrollView style={styles.stepContainer}>
      <Typography variant="headlineMd" style={styles.modalTitle}>Step 4: Review & Confirm</Typography>
      <Card style={styles.reviewCard}>
        <Typography variant="headlineSm">Borrower: {getBorrowerName(selectedBorrowerId)}</Typography>
        <Typography variant="bodyMd">Product: {productType === 'instalment' ? 'Upfront Markup' : 'Monthly Bullet'}</Typography>
        <Typography variant="bodyMd">Principal: {formatINR(toPaise(parseInt(principal) || 0))}</Typography>
        <Typography variant="bodyMd">Total Repayment: {formatINR(totalRepayment)}</Typography>
      </Card>
      
      <Typography variant="labelLg" style={{marginTop: theme.spacing.lg, marginBottom: theme.spacing.sm}}>Repayment Schedule</Typography>
      {previewSchedule.map((kist, index) => (
        <View key={index} style={styles.scheduleRow}>
          <Typography variant="bodySm">#{kist.kistNumber} - {kist.dueDate}</Typography>
          <Typography variant="labelLg">{formatINR(kist.amountDue)}</Typography>
        </View>
      ))}

      <View style={[styles.row, {marginTop: theme.spacing.xl, marginBottom: 40}]}>
        <Button title="Back" variant="outline" onPress={() => setStep(3)} style={{flex: 1, marginRight: 8}} />
        <Button title="Confirm & Disburse" onPress={handleCreate} style={{flex: 1}} />
      </View>
    </ScrollView>
  );

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Typography variant="headlineMd" color={theme.colors.onPrimary}>Active Loans</Typography>
        <Button title="New Loan" onPress={() => { resetForm(); setModalVisible(true); }} style={styles.headerBtn} />
      </View>

      <FlatList
        data={data}
        keyExtractor={item => item.id}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <TouchableOpacity activeOpacity={0.7} onPress={() => router.push(`/loan/${item.id}`)}>
            <Card elevated style={styles.card}>
              <Typography variant="headlineSm">{formatINR(item.principal_paise as unknown as Paise)}</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>Borrower: {getBorrowerName(item.borrower_id)}</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodySm" color={item.status === 'ACTIVE' ? theme.colors.success : theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                Status: {item.status}
              </Typography>
            </Card>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
        ListEmptyComponent={() => (
          <View style={styles.empty}>
            {error ? (
              <Typography variant="bodyLg" color={theme.colors.error}>{error}</Typography>
            ) : (
              <Typography variant="bodyLg" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>No active loans</Typography>
            )}
          </View>
        )}
      />

      <Modal visible={isModalVisible} animationType="slide" presentationStyle="pageSheet">
        <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }} edges={['top']}>
          <KeyboardAvoidingView style={{ flex: 1 }} behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}>
          <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setModalVisible(false)}><Typography color={theme.colors.primary}>Cancel</Typography></TouchableOpacity>
            <Typography variant="labelLg">New Loan - Step {step}/4</Typography>
            <View style={{width: 40}} />
          </View>
          {step === 1 && renderStep1()}
          {step === 2 && renderStep2()}
          {step === 3 && renderStep3()}
          {step === 4 && renderStep4()}
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
        </SafeAreaView>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.background },
  header: {
    flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center',
    padding: theme.spacing.lg, backgroundColor: theme.colors.primary, paddingTop: 16
  },
  headerBtn: { backgroundColor: theme.colors.secondary, borderRadius: 8 },
  list: { padding: theme.spacing.md, gap: theme.spacing.md },
  card: { marginBottom: theme.spacing.sm },
  empty: { padding: theme.spacing.xl, alignItems: 'center' },
  modalHeader: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', padding: theme.spacing.md, borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: theme.colors.outline },
  stepContainer: { flex: 1, padding: theme.spacing.lg },
  modalTitle: { marginBottom: theme.spacing.lg },
  borrowerList: { maxHeight: 200, marginBottom: theme.spacing.md, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: theme.colors.outline, borderRadius: 8 },
  borrowerCard: { padding: 12, borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: theme.colors.outline },
  borrowerCardSelected: { backgroundColor: theme.colors.successContainer },
  productToggle: { flexDirection: 'row', marginTop: 8, marginBottom: 20 },
  productBtn: { flex: 1, padding: 12, alignItems: 'center', borderWidth: 1, borderColor: theme.colors.primary, borderRadius: 8, marginHorizontal: 4 },
  productBtnActive: { backgroundColor: theme.colors.primary },
  nextBtn: { marginTop: 'auto', marginBottom: 40 },
  row: { flexDirection: 'row', marginTop: theme.spacing.lg },
  reviewCard: { backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceTint, padding: 16, borderRadius: 8 },
  scheduleRow: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', paddingVertical: 8, borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: theme.colors.outline }
});

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { View, FlatList, StyleSheet, Alert, Modal, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform, TouchableOpacity } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Typography } from '../../ui/Typography';
import { Button } from '../../ui/Button';
import { Card } from '../../ui/Card';
import { Input } from '../../ui/Input';
import { theme } from '../../ui/theme';
import { useDatabase } from '../../core/database/DatabaseProvider';
import { Borrower } from '../../core/database/borrowerRepository';

export default function BorrowersScreen() {
  const { borrowers, loans, refreshVersion, triggerRefresh } = useDatabase();
  const [data, setData] = useState<Borrower[]>([]);
  const [isModalVisible, setModalVisible] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState('');

  const loadBorrowers = useCallback(async () => {
    if (borrowers) {
      const result = await borrowers.getAll();
      setData(result);
    }
  }, [borrowers]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    loadBorrowers();
  }, [loadBorrowers, refreshVersion]);

  const handleAdd = async () => {
    if (!name || !phone) {
      Alert.alert('Error', 'Please fill in all fields');
      return;
    }
    
    try {
      await borrowers!.create(name, phone);
      setModalVisible(false);
      setName('');
      setPhone('');
      triggerRefresh();
    } catch (e: any) {
      Alert.alert('Error', e.message);
    }
  };

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.container} edges={['top']}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <Typography variant="headlineMd" color={theme.colors.onPrimary}>Customers</Typography>
        <Button title="Add New" onPress={() => setModalVisible(true)} style={styles.headerBtn} />
      </View>

      <FlatList
        data={data}
        keyExtractor={item => item.id}
        contentContainerStyle={styles.list}
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <Card elevated style={styles.card}>
            <Typography variant="headlineSm">{item.name}</Typography>
            <Typography variant="bodyMd" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>{item.phone}</Typography>
          </Card>
        )}
        ListEmptyComponent={() => (
          <View style={styles.empty}>
            <Typography variant="bodyLg" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>No customers found</Typography>
          </View>
        )}
      />

      <Modal visible={isModalVisible} animationType="slide" presentationStyle="pageSheet">
        <SafeAreaView style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.background }} edges={['top']}>
        <KeyboardAvoidingView 
          style={{ flex: 1 }} 
          behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
        >
          <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
            <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setModalVisible(false)}><Typography color={theme.colors.primary}>Cancel</Typography></TouchableOpacity>
            <Typography variant="labelLg">New Customer</Typography>
            <View style={{width: 40}} />
          </View>
          <View style={styles.modalContainer}>
            <Typography variant="headlineMd" style={styles.modalTitle}>Borrower Details</Typography>
            
            <Input
              label="Full Name"
              placeholder="e.g. Ramesh Kumar"
              value={name}
              onChangeText={setName}
            />
            <Input
              label="Phone Number"
              placeholder="e.g. 9876543210"
              keyboardType="phone-pad"
              value={phone}
              onChangeText={setPhone}
            />
            
            <View style={styles.modalActions}>
              <Button 
                title="Save Customer" 
                style={styles.modalButton} 
                onPress={handleAdd} 
              />
            </View>
          </View>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
        </SafeAreaView>
      </Modal>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.background },
  header: {
    flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center',
    padding: theme.spacing.lg, backgroundColor: theme.colors.primary, paddingTop: 16
  },
  headerBtn: { backgroundColor: theme.colors.secondary, borderRadius: 8 },
  list: { padding: theme.spacing.md, gap: theme.spacing.md },
  card: { marginBottom: theme.spacing.sm },
  empty: { padding: theme.spacing.xl, alignItems: 'center' },
  modalHeader: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', padding: theme.spacing.md, borderBottomWidth: 1, borderBottomColor: theme.colors.outline },
  modalContainer: { flex: 1, padding: theme.spacing.xl, backgroundColor: theme.colors.background },
  modalTitle: { marginBottom: theme.spacing.xl },
  modalActions: { flexDirection: 'row', marginTop: theme.spacing.xl, gap: theme.spacing.md },
  modalButton: { flex: 1 }
});

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { View, StyleSheet, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity } from 'react-native';
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Typography } from '../../ui/Typography';
import { Card } from '../../ui/Card';
import { theme } from '../../ui/theme';
import { useDatabase } from '../../core/database/DatabaseProvider';
import { Borrower } from '../../core/database/borrowerRepository';
import { Loan } from '../../core/database/loanRepository';
import { formatINR, Paise } from '../../core/domain/money';

export default function BorrowerDetailsScreen() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams();
  const router = useRouter();
  const { borrowers, loans, refreshVersion } = useDatabase();
  
  const [borrower, setBorrower] = useState<Borrower | null>(null);
  const [borrowerLoans, setBorrowerLoans] = useState<Loan[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function load() {
      if (borrowers && loans && id) {
        const b = await borrowers.getById(id as string);
        setBorrower(b);
        const lns = await loans.getAll();
        setBorrowerLoans(lns.filter((l: Loan) => l.borrower_id === id));
      }
    }
    load();
  }, [id, borrowers, loans, refreshVersion]);

  if (!borrower) return null;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea}>
      <ScrollView style={styles.container} contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <Typography variant="headlineMd" color={theme.colors.onPrimary}>Borrower Profile</Typography>
        </View>
        
        <Card elevated style={styles.card}>
          <Typography variant="headlineSm">{borrower.name}</Typography>
          <Typography variant="bodyMd" color={theme.colors.onSurfaceVariant}>{borrower.phone}</Typography>
        </Card>
        
        <Typography variant="titleMd" style={styles.sectionTitle}>Associated Loans</Typography>
        
        {borrowerLoans.map(loan => (
          <TouchableOpacity key={loan.id} onPress={() => router.push(`/loan/${loan.id}` as any)}>
            <Card style={styles.loanCard}>
              <Typography variant="bodyLg">{loan.product_type === 'instalment' ? 'Monthly Kist' : 'Interest Only'}</Typography>
              <Typography variant="headlineSm" color={theme.colors.primary}>{formatINR(loan.principal_paise as Paise)}</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodySm" color={loan.status === 'ACTIVE' ? theme.colors.success : theme.colors.textSecondary}>
                {loan.status}
              </Typography>
            </Card>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safeArea: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.background },
  container: { flex: 1 },
  header: { padding: theme.spacing.lg, backgroundColor: theme.colors.primary },
  content: { padding: theme.spacing.lg },
  card: { padding: theme.spacing.lg, marginBottom: theme.spacing.xl },
  sectionTitle: { marginBottom: theme.spacing.md },
  loanCard: { marginBottom: theme.spacing.md, padding: theme.spacing.md }
});

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { View, StyleSheet, ScrollView } from 'react-native';
import { useLocalSearchParams, Stack } from 'expo-router';
import { Typography } from '../../ui/Typography';
import { Card } from '../../ui/Card';
import { theme } from '../../ui/theme';
import { useDatabase } from '../../core/database/DatabaseProvider';
import { Loan } from '../../core/database/loanRepository';

export default function LoanDetailsScreen() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams();
  const { loans } = useDatabase();
  const [loan, setLoan] = useState<Loan | null>(null);
  const [schedule, setSchedule] = useState<any[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function load() {
      if (loans && typeof id === 'string') {
        const l = await loans.getById(id);
        setLoan(l);
        if (l) {
          const s = await loans.getSchedule(id);
          setSchedule(s);
        }
      }
    }
    load();
  }, [id, loans]);

  if (!loan) {
    return (
      <View style={styles.center}>
        <Typography>Loading...</Typography>
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <ScrollView style={styles.container} contentContainerStyle={{ padding: theme.spacing.lg, paddingBottom: 40 }}>
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: 'Loan Details' }} />
      
      <Card style={styles.headerCard}>
        <Typography variant="headlineMd">Principal: {loan.principal_paise / 100} INR</Typography>
        <Typography variant="bodyLg">Status: {loan.status}</Typography>
        <Typography variant="bodyMd" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>
          Created: {new Date(loan.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}
        </Typography>
      </Card>

      <Typography variant="headlineSm" style={styles.sectionTitle}>Repayment Schedule</Typography>
      
      {schedule.map((kist) => (
        <Card key={kist.id} style={styles.kistCard}>
          <View style={styles.kistHeader}>
            <Typography variant="labelLg">Kist {kist.installment_number}</Typography>
            <Typography variant="labelLg" color={kist.status === 'PAID' ? theme.colors.success : theme.colors.warning}>
              {kist.status}
            </Typography>
          </View>
          
          <View style={styles.kistRow}>
            <Typography variant="bodyMd">Due Date:</Typography>
            <Typography variant="bodyMd">{kist.effective_due_date}</Typography>
          </View>
          
          <View style={styles.kistRow}>
            <Typography variant="bodyMd">Total Due:</Typography>
            <Typography variant="bodyMd">{kist.total_due_paise / 100} INR</Typography>
          </View>
          
          <View style={styles.kistRow}>
            <Typography variant="bodyMd">Paid:</Typography>
            <Typography variant="bodyMd">{(kist.principal_paid_paise + kist.markup_paid_paise) / 100} INR</Typography>
          </View>
        </Card>
      ))}
    </ScrollView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: {
    flex: 1,
    backgroundColor: theme.colors.background,
  },
  center: {
    flex: 1,
    justifyContent: 'center',
    alignItems: 'center',
  },
  headerCard: {
    marginBottom: theme.spacing.xl,
    backgroundColor: theme.colors.primaryContainer,
  },
  sectionTitle: {
    marginBottom: theme.spacing.md,
  },
  kistCard: {
    marginBottom: theme.spacing.sm,
  },
  kistHeader: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    marginBottom: theme.spacing.sm,
    borderBottomWidth: 1,
    borderBottomColor: theme.colors.outline,
    paddingBottom: theme.spacing.xs,
  },
  kistRow: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
    marginBottom: 4,
  }
});

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { View, StyleSheet, ScrollView } from 'react-native';
import { useLocalSearchParams, useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Typography } from '../../../ui/Typography';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';
import { theme } from '../../../ui/theme';
import { useDatabase } from '../../../core/database/DatabaseProvider';
import { Loan } from '../../../core/database/loanRepository';
import { formatINR, Paise } from '../../../core/domain/money';

export default function LoanDetailsScreen() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams();
  const { loans, transactions, refreshVersion } = useDatabase();
  
  const [loan, setLoan] = useState<Loan | null>(null);
  const [earnings, setEarnings] = useState<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function load() {
      if (loans && transactions && id) {
        const lns = await loans.getAll();
        const l = lns.find(x => x.id === id);
        if (l) {
          setLoan(l);
          const summary = await transactions.getEarningsSummary({ loanId: l.id });
          setEarnings(summary);
        }
      }
    }
    load();
  }, [id, loans, transactions, refreshVersion]);

  if (!loan) return null;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea}>
      <ScrollView style={styles.container} contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        <View style={styles.header}>
          <Typography variant="headlineMd" color={theme.colors.onPrimary}>Loan Details</Typography>
        </View>
        
        <Card elevated style={styles.card}>
          <Typography variant="labelLg">Principal</Typography>
          <Typography variant="headlineMd" color={theme.colors.primary}>{formatINR(loan.principal_paise as Paise)}</Typography>
          <Typography variant="bodyMd">Status: {loan.status}</Typography>
        </Card>

        {earnings && (
          <Card style={styles.earningsCard}>
            <Typography variant="titleMd" style={{marginBottom: 10}}>Earnings & Collection Summary</Typography>
            <View style={styles.row}>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">Principal Returned:</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">{formatINR(earnings.principalReturned)}</Typography>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.row}>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">Markup Received:</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">{formatINR(earnings.markupReceived)}</Typography>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.row}>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">Penalty Received:</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">{formatINR(earnings.penaltyReceived)}</Typography>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.row, {marginTop: 10, paddingTop: 10, borderTopWidth: 1, borderColor: theme.colors.outlineVariant}]}>
              <Typography variant="labelLg">Total Actual Earnings:</Typography>
              <Typography variant="labelLg" color={theme.colors.success}>{formatINR(earnings.totalEarnings)}</Typography>
            </View>
          </Card>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safeArea: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.background },
  container: { flex: 1 },
  header: { padding: theme.spacing.lg, backgroundColor: theme.colors.primary, paddingTop: 40 },
  content: { padding: theme.spacing.lg },
  card: { padding: theme.spacing.lg, marginBottom: theme.spacing.xl },
  earningsCard: { padding: theme.spacing.md, backgroundColor: theme.colors.surfaceTint },
  row: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 4 }
});

The code currently has no automated test that can reproduce either reported symptom. Existing allocation tests are pure in-memory tests; the “integration” repository test uses a mock database and does not exercise Expo SQLite, rollback, concurrency, or two-loan isolation. I’ll still run the existing suite and static gates, then distinguish verified defects from hypotheses that need device logs/reproduction.

170:   * db.withExclusiveTransactionAsync(async (txn) => {
171:   *   await txn.execAsync('UPDATE test SET name = "aaa"');

784: * A new connection specific used for [`withExclusiveTransactionAsync`](#withexclusivetransactionasynctask).
9:3. Persistent protection against duplicate payment entries across application crashes, background process kills, or rapid user retries.
19:We implement a normalized relational financial schema using SQLite. The design combines **immutable transaction records** with **materialized balance columns**, governed by database-level constraints, atomic transaction blocks, and deterministic in-memory financial domain logic.
41:  Never execute manual SQL strings such as `BEGIN TRANSACTION`, `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, or `COMMIT` directly inside `withExclusiveTransactionAsync`. In `expo-sqlite`, transaction lifecycle and locking are managed by the native bridge wrapper; manual transaction SQL causes internal state collisions and crashes.
167:- If `accrued_penalty_action = 'WAIVE'`, a noncash `PENALTY_WAIVER` transaction row is inserted in the same atomic transaction, reducing `outstanding_penalty_paise`.
200:   In V1, **only the single latest active payment on a specific loan account may be reversed**.
241:  - Zero duplicate penalties or double-collections via database-enforced idempotency keys.
245:  - Reversals in V1 are constrained to the latest payment (LIFO), preventing arbitrary out-of-order historical edits.

import { SQLiteDatabase } from 'expo-sqlite';
import * as crypto from 'expo-crypto';
import { allocatePayment, KistAllocationState } from '../domain/allocation';
import { Paise } from '../domain/money';

export class TransactionRepository {
  constructor(private db: SQLiteDatabase) {}

  async recordCashPayment(
    loanId: string, 
    paymentAmount: Paise, 
    operationId: string,
    effectiveDate: string
  ) {
      let returnResult: any = null;

      await this.db.withExclusiveTransactionAsync(async (tx) => {
        // Idempotency check
        const existing = await tx.getFirstAsync(`SELECT * FROM transactions WHERE operation_id = ?`, [operationId]);
        if (existing) {
          returnResult = existing;
          return;
        }
        
        // Ensure schedule is up to date (for bullet loans)
        const { LoanRepository } = require('./loanRepository');
        const loanRepo = new LoanRepository(tx);
        await loanRepo.extendScheduleIfNeeded(loanId, tx);

        // Load current kists
        const dbKists = await tx.getAllAsync<any>(
          `SELECT * FROM repayment_schedules WHERE loan_id = ? ORDER BY installment_number ASC`, 
          [loanId]
        );
        
        const kists: KistAllocationState[] = dbKists.map(k => ({
          kistNumber: k.installment_number,
          amountDue: k.total_due_paise as Paise,
          principalDue: k.principal_component_paise as Paise,
          markupDue: k.markup_component_paise as Paise,
          amountPaid: (k.principal_paid_paise + k.markup_paid_paise) as Paise,
          principalPaid: k.principal_paid_paise as Paise,
          markupPaid: k.markup_paid_paise as Paise,
          status: k.status,
          isDue: k.effective_due_date <= effectiveDate
        }));
        
        // Assess penalties up to effectiveDate
        await this.assessPenalties(loanId, effectiveDate, tx);

        // Fetch outstanding penalty
        // Outstanding penalty = (Total PENALTY_ASSESSMENT amounts) - (Total allocated_penalty_paise from all collections)
        const penaltyAssessments = await tx.getFirstAsync<any>(
          `SELECT SUM(amount_paise) as total FROM transactions WHERE loan_id = ? AND transaction_type = 'PENALTY_ASSESSMENT'`,
          [loanId]
        );
        const penaltyPaid = await tx.getFirstAsync<any>(
          `SELECT SUM(allocated_penalty_paise) as total FROM transactions WHERE loan_id = ? AND transaction_type = 'COLLECTION'`,
          [loanId]
        );
        
        const totalPenaltyAssessed = (penaltyAssessments?.total || 0) as number;
        const totalPenaltyPaid = (penaltyPaid?.total || 0) as number;
        const outstandingPenalty = Math.max(0, totalPenaltyAssessed - totalPenaltyPaid) as Paise;
  
        const result = allocatePayment({ paymentAmount, kists, outstandingPenalty });
        
        const transactionId = crypto.randomUUID();
        const createdAt = new Date().toISOString();
        
        let allocatedPrincipal = 0;
        let allocatedMarkup = 0;
        let allocatedPenalty = 0;
        
        for (const a of result.allocations) {
          allocatedPrincipal += a.allocatedToPrincipal;
          allocatedMarkup += a.allocatedToMarkup;
          allocatedPenalty += a.allocatedToPenalty;
        }
        
        await tx.runAsync(`
          INSERT INTO transactions (
            id, operation_id, loan_id, transaction_type, is_cash, payment_method, 
            amount_paise, allocated_principal_paise, allocated_markup_paise, 
            allocated_penalty_paise, unallocated_surplus_paise, effective_date, created_at
          ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        `, [
          transactionId, operationId, loanId, 'COLLECTION', 1, 'CASH',
          paymentAmount, allocatedPrincipal, allocatedMarkup, allocatedPenalty,
          result.unallocatedFunds, effectiveDate, createdAt
        ]);
        
        // Update repayment schedules
        const updateStmt = await tx.prepareAsync(`
          UPDATE repayment_schedules
          SET principal_paid_paise = ?, markup_paid_paise = ?, status = ?
          WHERE loan_id = ? AND installment_number = ?
        `);
        
        try {
          for (const k of result.updatedKists) {
            // Only update if changed
            const orig = kists.find(o => o.kistNumber === k.kistNumber)!;
            if (k.amountPaid !== orig.amountPaid || k.status !== orig.status) {
               await updateStmt.executeAsync([
                 k.principalPaid, k.markupPaid, k.status || 'PENDING', loanId, k.kistNumber
               ]);
            }
          }
        } finally {
          await updateStmt.finalizeAsync();
        }
        // Check if loan should be closed
        const loan = await tx.getFirstAsync<any>(`SELECT product_type FROM loans WHERE id = ?`, [loanId]);
        
        let allKistsPaid = true;
        for (const k of result.updatedKists) {
          if (k.status !== 'PAID') {
            allKistsPaid = false;
            break;
          }
        }
        
        // For instalment loans, if all kists are paid and no outstanding penalty remains, settle the loan
        // For bullet loans, they require an explicit principal repayment transaction to settle (not yet implemented in this method)
        if (loan && loan.product_type === 'instalment' && allKistsPaid && result.remainingPenalty <= 0) {
           await tx.runAsync(`UPDATE loans SET status = 'SETTLED' WHERE id = ?`, [loanId]);
        }
        
        returnResult = { success: true, transactionId, result };
      });
      
      return returnResult;
  }

  async previewPenalties(loanId: string, currentDate: string): Promise<number> {
    const loan = await this.db.getFirstAsync<any>(`SELECT * FROM loans WHERE id = ?`, [loanId]);
    if (!loan || loan.status !== 'ACTIVE') return 0;

    const { LoanRepository } = require('./loanRepository');
    const loanRepo = new LoanRepository(this.db);
    await loanRepo.extendScheduleIfNeeded(loanId);

    const dbKists = await this.db.getAllAsync<any>(
      `SELECT * FROM repayment_schedules WHERE loan_id = ? ORDER BY installment_number ASC`,
      [loanId]
    );

    const assessments = await this.db.getAllAsync<any>(
      `SELECT effective_date FROM transactions WHERE loan_id = ? AND transaction_type = 'PENALTY_ASSESSMENT'`,
      [loanId]
    );

    const assessedDates = new Set<string>(assessments.map(a => a.effective_date));

    // Dynamic import to avoid circular dep
    const { calculateNewPenalties } = require('../domain/penalty');
    const { toPaise } = require('../domain/money');

    const state = {
      loanId,
      rule: { dailyRatePaise: toPaise(100) },
      kists: dbKists.map(k => ({
        kistNumber: k.installment_number,
        dueDate: k.effective_due_date,
        amountDue: k.total_due_paise,
        amountPaid: k.principal_paid_paise + k.markup_paid_paise,
        isFullyPaid: k.status === 'PAID'
      })),
      assessedDates
    };

    const { newPenaltyAmount } = calculateNewPenalties(state, currentDate);
    return newPenaltyAmount;
  }

  async assessPenalties(loanId: string, currentDate: string, tx: any = this.db): Promise<number> {
    const loan = await tx.getFirstAsync(`SELECT * FROM loans WHERE id = ?`, [loanId]);
    if (!loan || loan.status !== 'ACTIVE') return 0;

    const { LoanRepository } = require('./loanRepository');
    const loanRepo = new LoanRepository(tx);
    await loanRepo.extendScheduleIfNeeded(loanId, tx);

    const dbKists = await tx.getAllAsync(
      `SELECT * FROM repayment_schedules WHERE loan_id = ? ORDER BY installment_number ASC`,
      [loanId]
    );

    const assessments = await tx.getAllAsync(
      `SELECT effective_date FROM transactions WHERE loan_id = ? AND transaction_type = 'PENALTY_ASSESSMENT'`,
      [loanId]
    );

    const assessedDates = new Set<string>(assessments.map(a => a.effective_date));

    // Dynamic import to avoid circular dep if any, or just require
    const { calculateNewPenalties, LoanPenaltyState } = require('../domain/penalty');
    const { toPaise } = require('../domain/money');

    const state = {
      loanId,
      rule: { dailyRatePaise: toPaise(100) },
      kists: dbKists.map(k => ({
        kistNumber: k.installment_number,
        dueDate: k.effective_due_date,
        amountDue: k.total_due_paise,
        amountPaid: k.principal_paid_paise + k.markup_paid_paise,
        isFullyPaid: k.status === 'PAID'
      })),
      assessedDates
    };

    const { newPenaltyAmount, newAssessedDates } = calculateNewPenalties(state, currentDate);

    if (newPenaltyAmount > 0) {
      for (const date of newAssessedDates) {
        const transactionId = crypto.randomUUID();
        const operationId = `penalty-${loanId}-${date}`;
        
        // Check if it already exists to be safe
        const existing = await tx.getFirstAsync(`SELECT id FROM transactions WHERE operation_id = ?`, [operationId]);
        if (!existing) {
          await tx.runAsync(`
            INSERT INTO transactions (
              id, operation_id, loan_id, transaction_type, is_cash, payment_method, 
              amount_paise, allocated_principal_paise, allocated_markup_paise, 
              allocated_penalty_paise, unallocated_surplus_paise, effective_date, created_at
            ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
          `, [
            transactionId, operationId, loanId, 'PENALTY_ASSESSMENT', 0, 'SYSTEM',
            toPaise(100), 0, 0, 0, 0, date, new Date().toISOString()
          ]);
        }
      }
    }

    return newPenaltyAmount;
  }

import { SQLiteDatabase } from 'expo-sqlite';
import * as crypto from 'expo-crypto';
import { ScheduleOptions, generateSchedule } from '../domain/schedule';

export type Loan = {
  id: string;
  borrower_id: string;
  principal_paise: number;
  markup_factor: number;
  term_months: number;
  start_date: string;
  due_day: number;
  product_type: string;
  status: string;
  created_at: string;
};

export class LoanRepository {
  constructor(private db: SQLiteDatabase) {}

  async createLoanWithSchedule(
    borrowerId: string, 
    options: ScheduleOptions
  ): Promise<Loan> {
    const loanId = crypto.randomUUID();
    const created_at = new Date().toISOString();
    
    const schedule = generateSchedule(options);

    await this.db.withExclusiveTransactionAsync(async (tx) => {
      await this.db.runAsync(
        `INSERT INTO loans (id, borrower_id, principal_paise, markup_factor, term_months, start_date, due_day, product_type, created_at)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [loanId, borrowerId, options.principal, options.markupFactor, options.termMonths, options.startDate, options.dueDay, options.productType, created_at]
      );
      
      const statement = await this.db.prepareAsync(`
        INSERT INTO repayment_schedules 
        (id, loan_id, installment_number, original_due_date, effective_due_date, principal_component_paise, markup_component_paise, total_due_paise)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `);
      
      try {
        for (const kist of schedule) {
          await statement.executeAsync([
            crypto.randomUUID(),
            loanId,
            kist.kistNumber,
            kist.dueDate,
            kist.dueDate,
            kist.principalDue || 0,
            kist.markupDue || 0,
            kist.amountDue
          ]);
        }
      } finally {
        await statement.finalizeAsync();
      }
    });

    return (await this.getById(loanId))!;
  }

  async getAll(): Promise<Loan[]> {
    return await this.db.getAllAsync<Loan>(`SELECT * FROM loans ORDER BY created_at DESC`);
  }
  
  async getByBorrowerId(borrowerId: string): Promise<Loan[]> {
    return await this.db.getAllAsync<Loan>(`SELECT * FROM loans WHERE borrower_id = ? ORDER BY created_at DESC`, [borrowerId]);
  }

  async getById(id: string): Promise<Loan | null> {
    return await this.db.getFirstAsync<Loan>(`SELECT * FROM loans WHERE id = ?`, [id]);
  }
  
  async getSchedule(loanId: string) {
    await this.extendScheduleIfNeeded(loanId);
    return await this.db.getAllAsync(`
      SELECT * FROM repayment_schedules 
      WHERE loan_id = ? 
      ORDER BY installment_number ASC
    `, [loanId]);
  }

  async extendScheduleIfNeeded(loanId: string, tx: any = this.db) {
    const loan = await tx.getFirstAsync(`SELECT * FROM loans WHERE id = ?`, [loanId]);
    if (!loan || loan.product_type !== 'bullet' || loan.status !== 'ACTIVE') return;

    const kists = await tx.getAllAsync(`
      SELECT * FROM repayment_schedules 
      WHERE loan_id = ? 
      ORDER BY installment_number ASC
    `, [loanId]);
    
    if (kists.length === 0) return;
    const lastKist = kists[kists.length - 1];
    const currentDate = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
    
    // If the last kist's due date is in the past, generate new ones until we pass currentDate
    if (lastKist.effective_due_date < currentDate) {
      // dynamic import for schedule util if needed, but it's not exported yet? Wait, I exported it.
      const { calculateNextDueDate } = require('../domain/schedule');
      const { toPaise } = require('../domain/money');
      
      const totalMarkup = loan.principal_paise * loan.markup_factor;
      const markupDuePerMonth = Math.floor(totalMarkup / loan.term_months);
      
      let nextInstalmentNumber = lastKist.installment_number + 1;
      let monthsToAddFromStart = nextInstalmentNumber;
      
      let nextDueDate = calculateNextDueDate(loan.start_date, monthsToAddFromStart, loan.due_day);
      
      const statement = await tx.prepareAsync(`
        INSERT INTO repayment_schedules 
        (id, loan_id, installment_number, original_due_date, effective_due_date, principal_component_paise, markup_component_paise, total_due_paise)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      `);
      
      try {
        while (nextDueDate <= currentDate || nextInstalmentNumber === lastKist.installment_number + 1 /* always add at least one if we entered */) {
          await statement.executeAsync([
            crypto.randomUUID(),
            loanId,
            nextInstalmentNumber,
            nextDueDate,
            nextDueDate,
            0,
            markupDuePerMonth,
            markupDuePerMonth
          ]);
          
          if (nextDueDate > currentDate) break;
          
          nextInstalmentNumber++;
          monthsToAddFromStart++;
          nextDueDate = calculateNextDueDate(loan.start_date, monthsToAddFromStart, loan.due_day);
        }
      } finally {
        await statement.finalizeAsync();
      }
    }
  }
}

import { SQLiteDatabase } from 'expo-sqlite';

export async function runMigrations(db: SQLiteDatabase) {
  await db.withExclusiveTransactionAsync(async (tx) => {
    // 1. Borrowers table
    await db.execAsync(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS borrowers (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
        name TEXT NOT NULL,
        phone TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL
      );
    `);

    // 2. Loans table
    await db.execAsync(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS loans (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
        borrower_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        principal_paise INTEGER NOT NULL,
        markup_factor REAL NOT NULL,
        term_months INTEGER NOT NULL,
        start_date TEXT NOT NULL,
        due_day INTEGER NOT NULL,
        product_type TEXT NOT NULL,
        status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        FOREIGN KEY (borrower_id) REFERENCES borrowers(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
      );
    `);

    // 3. Repayment Schedules
    await db.execAsync(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS repayment_schedules (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
        loan_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        installment_number INTEGER NOT NULL,
        original_due_date TEXT NOT NULL,
        effective_due_date TEXT NOT NULL,
        principal_component_paise INTEGER NOT NULL,
        markup_component_paise INTEGER NOT NULL,
        total_due_paise INTEGER NOT NULL,
        principal_paid_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        markup_paid_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
        FOREIGN KEY (loan_id) REFERENCES loans(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
      );
    `);

    // 4. Transactions table (Fixing the duplicate PK defect)
    await db.execAsync(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS transactions (
        id TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        operation_id TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        loan_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        sequence_number INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
        transaction_type TEXT NOT NULL,
        is_cash INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
        payment_method TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'CASH',
        amount_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        allocated_principal_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        allocated_markup_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        allocated_penalty_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        unallocated_surplus_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        forgiven_principal_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        forgiven_markup_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        forgiven_penalty_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        effective_date TEXT NOT NULL,
        created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        FOREIGN KEY (loan_id) REFERENCES loans(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
      );
    `);

    // Prevent transactions update/delete
    await db.execAsync(`
      CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS prevent_transactions_update
      BEFORE UPDATE ON transactions
      BEGIN
        SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'Transactions table is append-only: updates are prohibited');
      END;
    `);

    await db.execAsync(`
      CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS prevent_transactions_delete
      BEFORE DELETE ON transactions
      BEGIN
        SELECT RAISE(ABORT, 'Transactions table is append-only: deletions are prohibited');
      END;
    `);

    // 5. Schedule Modifications
    await db.execAsync(`
      CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schedule_modifications (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL,
        loan_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        kist_id TEXT NOT NULL,
        original_due_date TEXT NOT NULL,
        prior_effective_due_date TEXT NOT NULL,
        revised_effective_due_date TEXT NOT NULL,
        changed_at TEXT NOT NULL,
        reason TEXT,
        accrued_penalty_action TEXT NOT NULL,
        waived_penalty_paise INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
        operation_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
        FOREIGN KEY (kist_id) REFERENCES repayment_schedules(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT
      );
    `);
  });
}

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import { View, StyleSheet, ScrollView, TouchableOpacity } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useRouter } from 'expo-router';
import { Typography } from '../../ui/Typography';
import { theme } from '../../ui/theme';
import { useDatabase } from '../../core/database/DatabaseProvider';
import { Loan } from '../../core/database/loanRepository';
import { formatINR, Paise } from '../../core/domain/money';

export default function HomeScreen() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { error, loans, transactions, refreshVersion } = useDatabase();
  const [activeLoans, setActiveLoans] = useState<Loan[]>([]);
  const [totalOut, setTotalOut] = useState<Paise>(0 as Paise);
  const [collected, setCollected] = useState<Paise>(0 as Paise);
  const [overdue, setOverdue] = useState<Paise>(0 as Paise);

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
    if (loans) {
      const allLoans = await loans.getAll();
      const active = allLoans.filter(l => l.status === 'ACTIVE');
      setActiveLoans(active);
      
      const total = active.reduce((sum, l) => sum + l.principal_paise, 0) as Paise;
      setTotalOut(total);
    }
    if (transactions) {
      const summary = await transactions.getEarningsSummary();
      // Collected = principalReturned + markupReceived + penaltyReceived
      const totalCollected = summary.principalReturned + summary.markupReceived + summary.penaltyReceived;
      setCollected(totalCollected as Paise);
      // For overdue, just sum outstanding penalties and we could also query unpaid kists that are due.
      setOverdue(summary.outstandingPenalty as Paise);
    }
  }, [loans, transactions]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    loadDashboard();
  }, [loadDashboard, refreshVersion]);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea}>
      <View style={styles.header}>
        <View>
          <Typography variant="headlineSm" color={theme.colors.onPrimary} style={styles.brand}>CredKeep</Typography>
          <View style={styles.badgeRow}>
            <View style={styles.dot} />
            <Typography variant="labelSm" color={theme.colors.secondaryFixed}>OFFLINE LEDGER</Typography>
          </View>
        </View>
        <View style={styles.avatar} />
      </View>
      
      <ScrollView style={styles.container} contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        <View style={styles.localModeStrip}>
          <Typography variant="labelLg" color={theme.colors.surfaceContainerHighest}>Local Ledger Mode</Typography>
          <Typography variant="labelLg" color={theme.colors.secondaryFixed}>100% On-Device</Typography>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.heroCard}>
          <View style={styles.heroHeader}>
            <Typography variant="labelLg" color={theme.colors.secondaryFixed}>TOTAL OUTSTANDING</Typography>
            <View style={styles.heroBadge}>
              <Typography variant="labelLg" color={theme.colors.surfaceContainerHighest}>{activeLoans.length} active</Typography>
            </View>
          </View>
          <Typography variant="rupeeDisplay" color={theme.colors.onPrimary} style={styles.heroValue}>
            {formatINR(totalOut)}
          </Typography>
          <Typography variant="bodyLg" color={theme.colors.onPrimaryContainer}>
            Total principal deployed
          </Typography>

          <View style={styles.metricGrid}>
            <View style={styles.metricItem}>
              <Typography variant="labelLg" color={theme.colors.onPrimaryContainer}>Due Today</Typography>
              <Typography variant="headlineSm" color={theme.colors.onPrimary}>â‚¹0</Typography>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.metricItem, styles.metricBorder]}>
              <Typography variant="labelLg" color={theme.colors.onPrimaryContainer}>Collected</Typography>
              <Typography variant="headlineSm" color={theme.colors.successContainer}>{formatINR(collected)}</Typography>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.metricItem, styles.metricBorder]}>
              <Typography variant="labelLg" color={theme.colors.onPrimaryContainer}>Overdue</Typography>
              <Typography variant="headlineSm" color={theme.colors.error}>{formatINR(overdue)}</Typography>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>

        {error && (
          <View style={styles.errorCard}>
            <Typography variant="headlineSm" color={theme.colors.error}>Database Error</Typography>
            <Typography variant="bodyMd" color={theme.colors.error}>{error.message}</Typography>
          </View>
        )}

        <View style={styles.actionGrid}>
          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.actionBtn1} onPress={() => router.push('/loans')}>
            <View style={styles.actionIcon1} />
            <View>
              <Typography variant="headlineSm" color={theme.colors.onPrimary}>+ New Loan</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd" color={theme.colors.onPrimaryContainer}>Create borrower entry</Typography>
            </View>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity style={styles.actionBtn2} onPress={() => router.push('/collections')}>
            <View style={styles.actionIcon2} />
            <View>
              <Typography variant="headlineSm" color={theme.colors.onSecondary}>âœ“ Record Payment</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd" color={theme.colors.secondaryFixed}>Update ledger passbook</Typography>
            </View>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
        
        <View style={styles.followUpHeader}>
          <Typography variant="headlineSm" color={theme.colors.textPrimary}>Follow-ups Required</Typography>
          <View style={styles.followUpBadge}>
            <Typography variant="labelLg" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>0 Active</Typography>
          </View>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.footer}>
          <Typography variant="bodyMd" color={theme.colors.textSecondary}>All ledger data stored locally on device â€¢ Offline mode</Typography>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safeArea: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.primaryContainer },
  container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.surface },

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { View, StyleSheet, ScrollView } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Typography } from '../../ui/Typography';
import { Card } from '../../ui/Card';
import { theme } from '../../ui/theme';
import { useDatabase } from '../../core/database/DatabaseProvider';
import { formatINR } from '../../core/domain/money';

export default function ReportsScreen() {
  const { transactions, refreshVersion } = useDatabase();
  const [globalEarnings, setGlobalEarnings] = useState<any>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function load() {
      if (transactions) {
        // null filters = global sum
        const summary = await transactions.getEarningsSummary();
        setGlobalEarnings(summary);
      }
    }
    load();
  }, [transactions, refreshVersion]);

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.safeArea}>
      <ScrollView style={styles.container} contentContainerStyle={styles.content}>
        <Typography variant="headlineMd" style={styles.title}>Earnings & Reports</Typography>
        
        {globalEarnings && (
          <Card elevated style={styles.card}>
            <Typography variant="titleMd" style={{marginBottom: 10}}>Global Actual Cash Earnings</Typography>
            <View style={styles.row}>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">Principal Returned:</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">{formatINR(globalEarnings.principalReturned)}</Typography>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.row}>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">Contractual Markup Received:</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">{formatINR(globalEarnings.markupReceived)}</Typography>
            </View>
            <View style={styles.row}>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">Late Penalties Received:</Typography>
              <Typography variant="bodyMd">{formatINR(globalEarnings.penaltyReceived)}</Typography>
            </View>
            <View style={[styles.row, {marginTop: 10, paddingTop: 10, borderTopWidth: 1, borderColor: theme.colors.outlineVariant}]}>
              <Typography variant="labelLg">Total Actual Earnings:</Typography>
              <Typography variant="labelLg" color={theme.colors.success}>{formatINR(globalEarnings.totalEarnings)}</Typography>
            </View>
            
            <View style={{marginTop: 20}}>
              <Typography variant="labelMd" color={theme.colors.error}>Outstanding / Uncollected</Typography>
              <View style={styles.row}>
                <Typography variant="bodySm" color={theme.colors.error}>Outstanding Penalties:</Typography>
                <Typography variant="bodySm" color={theme.colors.error}>{formatINR(globalEarnings.outstandingPenalty)}</Typography>
              </View>
            </View>
          </Card>
        )}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  safeArea: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.colors.background },
  container: { flex: 1 },
  content: { padding: theme.spacing.lg, paddingTop: 40 },
  title: { marginBottom: theme.spacing.xl },
  card: { padding: theme.spacing.lg },
  row: { flexDirection: 'row', justifyContent: 'space-between', marginBottom: 6 }
});

import { LoanRepository } from '../core/database/loanRepository';
import { TransactionRepository } from '../core/database/transactionRepository';
import { toPaise } from '../core/domain/money';

describe('Cross-Loan Isolation', () => {
  let mockDb: any;
  let loanRepo: LoanRepository;
  let txRepo: TransactionRepository;
  let queries: any[] = [];
  let executionLog: any[] = [];
  let runAsyncCallbacks: any[] = [];

  beforeEach(() => {
    queries = [];
    executionLog = [];
    runAsyncCallbacks = [];
    
    mockDb = {
      withExclusiveTransactionAsync: jest.fn(async (cb) => {
        // mock tx is the same as db here for test tracking, but in reality expo passes a tx object
        const tx = { ...mockDb };
        await cb(tx);
      }),
      runAsync: jest.fn(async (query, params) => {
        queries.push({ query, params });
        executionLog.push('runAsync');
      }),
      prepareAsync: jest.fn(async (query) => {
        return {
          executeAsync: jest.fn(async (params) => {
            queries.push({ query, params });
            executionLog.push('executeAsync');
          }),
          finalizeAsync: jest.fn(async () => {})
        };
      }),
      getFirstAsync: jest.fn(async (query, params) => {
        if (query.includes('loans WHERE id = ?')) {
          return { id: params[0], product_type: 'instalment', status: 'ACTIVE' };
        }
        if (query.includes('operation_id = ?')) {
          return null;
        }
        if (query.includes('SUM(amount_paise)')) {
          return { total: 0 };
        }
        if (query.includes('SUM(allocated_penalty_paise)')) {
          return { total: 0 };
        }
        return null;
      }),
      getAllAsync: jest.fn(async (query, params) => {
        if (query.includes('repayment_schedules')) {
          return [
            {
              installment_number: 1,
              total_due_paise: 260000,
              principal_component_paise: 200000,
              markup_component_paise: 60000,
              principal_paid_paise: 0,
              markup_paid_paise: 0,
              effective_due_date: '2026-01-01',
              status: 'PENDING'
            }
          ];
        }
        if (query.includes('PENALTY_ASSESSMENT')) {
          return [];
        }
        return [];
      })
    };
    
    loanRepo = new LoanRepository(mockDb);
    txRepo = new TransactionRepository(mockDb);
  });

  it('Payment against Loan B should only update Loan B', async () => {
    const loanBId = 'loan-b';
    const amount = toPaise(2600); // 1 kist
    
    await txRepo.recordCashPayment(loanBId, amount, 'test-op', '2026-01-02');
    
    // Let's check which queries were run
    const updates = queries.filter(q => q.query.includes('UPDATE repayment_schedules'));
    
    expect(updates.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    for (const update of updates) {
      const loanIdIndex = update.params.length - 2; // Assuming WHERE loan_id = ? AND installment_number = ?
      expect(update.params[loanIdIndex]).toBe(loanBId);
    }
  });
});

const { runMigrations } = require('./src/core/database/schema');
const { LoanRepository } = require('./src/core/database/loanRepository');
const { TransactionRepository } = require('./src/core/database/transactionRepository');
const { toPaise } = require('./src/core/domain/money');

async function main() {
  const dbPath = 'test-db.sqlite';
  if (fs.existsSync(dbPath)) fs.unlinkSync(dbPath);
  
  // mock expo-sqlite
  const sqlite3 = require('sqlite3');
  // Wait, I can't easily mock expo-sqlite API here because expo-sqlite Next API is completely different from sqlite3.
  // I will write a test using Jest and run it through npm run test!
}
main();
